## Required checkpoint record for each bounded change

Do not use a whole functional-area row as an implementation batch. For each **individual page, handler or backend operation**, add a record with:

| Field | Required information |
| --- | --- |
| Identifier | Old page/route/operation, HTTP method and visible user action |
| Baseline | Revision, source file/function, representative input and actual output, focused test and result |
| Extract | New Python file, old dispatcher/delegation preserved, same test result |
| Move | Owning component and new path, same test result |
| Separate | Backend `impl/` callable, direct test plus old HTTP/UI regression result |
| Boundary | Approved Pydantic model and compatibility test, when applicable |
| HTTP | Owning FastAPI router, direct request tests and remaining old callers |
| UI | TypeScript page, browser-to-REST test and old Python page retirement |
| Gate | Latest passing commands/results, known failures, local checkpoint commit, status OPEN or VERIFIED |

**Order:** baseline → extract Python page → test → move module → test → separate backend → test → Pydantic boundary → test → FastAPI/component router → test → TypeScript page → test → remove obsolete code → full regression. One bounded change at a time. A stage may be recorded as N/A only with an explanation.

**No mass `git mv`, import-only success, test skipping or blanket parity claims.** The old `ThreadingHTTPServer` and Python rendering remain the working integration path until their specific replacements pass. Preserve existing behavior and files; any new EP-11 feature is separate work.

## Baseline command

**Fact.** Command: `make test` (runs `scripts/ci/checks/tests.py`, `unittest` discovery of `tests/test*.py`). Result: **416 PASS, 0 FAIL**. No skip lines were printed. Elapsed about 86 seconds. Exit code 0. This run did not execute `make cicd`, `make package`, `make package-studio`, `make package-curbcheck`, or the modules under `tests/cs/`.

`tests/cs/path_boundary.py`, `tests/cs/review_signature.py`, `tests/cs/check_result_integrity.py`, `tests/cs/git_provenance.py`, and `tests/cs/workspace_selector.py` define `unittest.TestCase` classes. Their filenames do not match `test*.py`. No other module imports them. They are **not** part of the 416.

## Where behavior lives today

**Fact.** One HTTP server: `http.server.ThreadingHTTPServer` in `app/server.py` (`make_handler`, `serve`, `main`). `python -m app` calls that `main`. There is no FastAPI application and no `models/pydantic/` package. `studio/pack/` holds the demo requirement page moved in MIG-62, the empty-folder POST moved in MIG-66, the extract job abort moved in MIG-67, the extract POST moved in MIG-68, the source-details model moved in MIG-69, the compare model moved in MIG-70, the consolidate model moved in MIG-71, the export model moved in MIG-72, the copy model moved in MIG-73, the extract-review model moved in MIG-74, the Pack sections model moved in MIG-75, the Pack sources model moved in MIG-76, the requirement editor moved in MIG-77, the demo requirement handler moved in MIG-78, and the pack-editor HTML moved in MIG-86. `studio/claim/` holds the Claims editor page moved in MIG-63, the try-discovery file model moved in MIG-79, the Claims model moved in MIG-80, the Discover model moved in MIG-81, the try-discovery HTML moved in MIG-83, the Discover page moved in MIG-84, and the researcher HTML moved in MIG-85. `studio/check/` holds the Check page moved in MIG-64 and the Check model moved in MIG-82. `studio/review/` holds the Review page moved in MIG-65. | Review view-model | `app/review_model.py` `_review_view` | The model calls `_sync_open_repo`. When `repo` or `repo_id` names an already registered id, that writes `open-repo.json`. `Handler.do_GET` calls `_sync_open_repo` before the page. After a normal return, `_review_get` calls `_door_chrome`, which calls `_sync_open_repo` again, so a registered id that renders the page is written three times. An unknown id raises `ModelError` before that write. `do_GET` discards the first raise. `/review` requires an open project. With no open repo, `do_GET` redirects to `/project` and the model does not run. That project page calls `ensure_repos_populated`, which writes `product_repos.json` only when the repo list is empty and a child directory has `curbpack.json`. An existing repo list is returned without that write. With an open repo and an unknown id, the model's call raises. The message does not contain `curbpack.json`, so `_review_get` re-raises and `do_GET` calls `_fail`. A query with no repo id skips the `open-repo.json` write. With an open repo, `_get` calls `_registered_sources` before the page. That calls `ensure_catalog`, which writes `registered_sources.json` only when that file is missing. An existing catalog is read and not rewritten. The redirect returns before `_get`, so it does not seed the catalog. A `ModelError` or `ValueError` whose text contains `curbpack.json` is caught in `_review_get`. That path calls `_sync_open_repo` again and renders the error page. A selected report calls `_report_presentation`, and a selected result calls `_raw_presentation`. Each calls `load_claims`. A claim that already has `checks` is only read. A stored claim is rewritten in place only when `check` is a non-empty string, `id` and `requirement_id` are strings, and `validate_claim` accepts the upgraded document. Otherwise the file stays. A GET with neither `report` nor `result` does not call `load_claims`. `_unchecked_for_repo` reads pack directories and does not write. `_review_file_view` reads one repo-relative file and does not write. `git_commit_signature` runs `git log` and does not write. Neither writes the working copy, the published release, or `curbpack.json`. `write_json` creates the parent directory when it is missing, writes a temporary file, and replaces the destination. If that write raises before the replace, the temporary file is removed and an existing `selection.json` stays. Each row is a function that returns HTML bytes for a GET. Owner is a **judgment** from the target architecture unless marked Open. Verification describes assertions inspected in this revision, not a coverage percentage.

Shared HTML helpers that later page extractions must keep importable from `app.pages`: `page`, `_flash`, `_reading`, `_current_project_label`, `progress_dialog`, and `repo_config_panel`. **Fact.** `app/cover.py` imports `page` and `_flash`. `studio/pack/requirement_page.py` imports `page`, `_flash`, and `_reading`. `app/studio_page.py` imports `page`, `_flash`, and `_current_project_label`. `studio/check/check_page.py` imports `page`, `_flash`, and `repo_config_panel`. `studio/claim/claims_page.py` imports `page`, `_flash`, and `repo_config_panel`. `studio/review/review_page.py` imports `page`, `_flash`, `_reading`, and `repo_config_panel`. `app/scaffold_page.py` imports `page` and `_flash`. `studio/pack/pages.py`, `requirements/ui/publication_page.py`, `studio/claim/discover_page.py`, `studio/claim/researcher.py`, and `studio/claim/try_discovery.py` import `page` and, for some of them, `progress_dialog` or `repo_config_panel`. `tests/test_review_compile.py` imports `review_page` from `studio.review.review_page`.

## HTTP handlers

Dispatcher: `app/server.py` `Handler.do_GET` / `do_POST`. Discover-claim and feedback paths return from `claims/ui/http.py` before the generic branches. Methods are the methods that branch handles. A path that is not listed falls through to "Unknown page." (GET) or `ModelError` "a known action" (POST).

Verification words: **Asserted** means a test issues that request and a later assertion checks the body, status, or a file that request writes. **Domain** means the underlying function is asserted and this URL is not. **Smoke** means the assertion is only a heading or the absence of a traceback. **None** means no such assertion was found.

| Method and path | Handler behavior (fact) | Domain functions used | Target owner (judgment) | Tests whose assertions were inspected |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| GET `/vendor/jsondiffpatch.js`, `/vendor/jsondiffpatch.css`, `/icons/curbpack-icon.svg` | `_send_vendor` reads the mapped file | none | `studio/pack/` pages for the diff assets; the icon is shell chrome | **Partial.** `test_requirements_compare.py` asserts the JS path is in the extract-review page, GET returns 200, and the body contains `jsondiffpatch`. **None** for the CSS or the SVG. The SVG file is tracked but omitted from the ZIP. |

Open-project gate, **fact:** `_requires_open_project` is true for `/`, `/requirements`, `/studio`, `/discover`, `/discover/repo`, `/check`, `/review`, `/requirement`, `/scaffold`, `/discover/run`, `/discover/jobs/…`, `/discover/claims…`, `/discover/feedback…`, and `/review/…`, `/studio/…`. It is false for `/requirements/…` subpaths other than exact `/requirements`, for `/project`, `/claims`, `/prepare`, `/select-pack`, and the demo `/publish` and `/seal-pack`. Failure redirects to `/project`. `test_project_page.py` asserts GET `/discover` with no open repo shows the project list. `test_cover_navigation.py` asserts `/check`, `/review`, `/studio`, `/requirement`, and `/scaffold` show "Registered projects" in that situation, and asserts `/claims`, `/requirements/compare`, and `/requirements/consolidate` still return a heading rather than a traceback.

The previous unverified route table in this file listed `/abort*` and `/try-discovery/file*` as if they were top-level paths. **Fact:** abort is only a suffix of the two job collections above. The try-discovery file page is `/requirements/requirement/{id}/try-discovery/file`.

## CLI and other entry points

| Command | Call path | Target | Tests inspected |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `make start` / `python -m app` | `app/__main__.py` → `app.server.main` → `--workspace` (default workspace), `--host` `127.0.0.1`, `--port` `8770` → `serve` | `studio/app.py` eventually starts the process and registers routers. Flags stay until a separate change. | `test_stop_app.py` asserts stop against a listener. No test asserts `main()`'s argparse itself. |
| `make stop` | `scripts/stop_app.py` | scripts, not a product component | **Asserted.** `test_stop_app.py` asserts lsof/ss/netstat naming and that a free port stays quiet. |
| `python -m checks` | `checks/__main__.py` `_cli`: `--defaults`, `--repo`, `--packs`, `--requirements`, `-j/--json`, `-o/--out`, `--skip` | `curbcheck/cli.py` → `curbcheck/api.py` | **Asserted.** `test_chk07_curbcheck.py` asserts defaults JSON, dated results, both `-j` and `-o`, exit 2 when a pack id is a path, and no file written for invalid `curbpack.json`. |
| `curbcheck-exist`, `curbcheck-contents`, `curbcheck-git`, `curbcheck-node` | `checks/bin/*` or argv0 dispatch in `checks/__main__.py` → `checks/toolmain.py` → `checks/commands.py` | `curbcheck/checks/` | **Asserted.** `test_curbcheck_package.py` asserts exist/contents through the tool entry, git fresh/author, and dep_ban. `test_check_dispatch.py` asserts a separate executable receives check id, repo, and args, and a missing tool fails closed while the next check still runs. `test_git_checks.py` and `test_m4_checks.py` assert the check results. |
| `compile-pdf.py` / `python -m` is not wired; the script calls `compile.cli.main` | `--report`, `--out` → `compile.pdf.compile_pdf` | `studio/compile/` | **Asserted.** `test_compile_pdf.py` asserts a readable PDF, an unchanged source report, no write when the parent is missing, and rejection of a changed claim. |
| `scripts/release/publish.py`, `verify.py`, `smoke.py` | CI release of built artifacts | scripts. Not Pack Publish. | **Asserted** in `test_release_publish.py` (tag, checksum notes, refusal of a run that has not passed). This is not the in-app `/requirements/publish` flow. |
| `scripts/check_story_plan.py` | plan-file structure check | not a product runtime | **Asserted** by `test_story_plan_contract.py`. Out of EP-02 product migration. |

No Pack, Claim, or Review CLI exists. EP-09 would add thin CLIs later. That is not this stage.

| `review/present.py` | Review read model | `studio/review/impl/` | Asserted through Review HTTP tests and `test_rev03_review.py` `test_seal_label_mapping` |
| `review/gitseal.py` | Git commit signature and claim signature reading | `studio/review/impl/` for review display; signature primitives can stay shared | `test_rev03_review.py`, `test_e2e_annex_vii.py`, `tests/cs/git_provenance.py` (not in `make test`) |
| `review/view_html.py` | Second Review renderer | No owner until something calls it. It is unused. | **None** |
| `compile/pdf.py`, `compile/cli.py` | PDF from a saved report | `studio/compile/` | `test_compile_pdf.py` |
| `scaffold/stub.py` | Write a stub file | Same late Studio component as the scaffold page. See Decided ownership. | `test_e2e_annex_vii.py` |
| `common/jsonio.py`, `common/store.py`, `common/validate.py`, `common/signing.py`, `common/keystore.py`, `common/canonical.py`, `common/paths.py`, `common/errors.py` | Shared IO, validation, signing, digests | `models/` only for the future Pydantic shapes of Packs v2. IO and signing stay callable from component `impl/`. Do not invent a service layer. | `test_jsonio.py`, `test_m1_requirement.py`, `test_requirements_publish.py`, `test_delivery.py` (paths) |
| `demo_setup.py`, `workflow.py` | Sample workspace and demo requirement reading | Keep with the demo routes they serve. Not new features. | `test_ui_smoke.py` uses `prepare_demo` |
| `scripts/build/`, `Makefile`, `.github/workflows/` | Package and CI | Stay scripts. Entrypoints change only when the process entry moves. | `test_delivery.py`, `test_installers.py`, `test_curbcheck_package.py` (unit-level; this baseline did not build native binaries) |

## Architecture deviations (verified)

These are differences between this tree and the target. They are expected before migration. They are not defects to fix in MIG-00.

1. **Fact.** Dispatch is still `app/server.py`. The demo requirement page moved in MIG-62 to `studio/pack/requirement_page.py`. The Claims editor page moved in MIG-63 to `studio/claim/claims_page.py`. The Check page moved in MIG-64 to `studio/check/check_page.py`. The Review page moved in MIG-65 to `studio/review/review_page.py`. The empty-folder POST moved in MIG-66 to `studio/pack/empty_folder.py`. The extract job abort moved in MIG-67 to `studio/pack/extract_abort.py`. The extract POST moved in MIG-68 to `studio/pack/extract_start.py`. The source-details model moved in MIG-69 to `studio/pack/inspect_model.py`. The compare model moved in MIG-70 to `studio/pack/pack_compare.py`. The consolidate model moved in MIG-71 to `studio/pack/pack_consolidate.py`. The export model moved in MIG-72 to `studio/pack/pack_export.py`. The copy model moved in MIG-73 to `studio/pack/copy_model.py`. The extract-review model moved in MIG-74 to `studio/pack/extract_review.py`. The Pack sections model moved in MIG-75 to `studio/pack/catalog_model.py`. The Pack sources model moved in MIG-76 to `studio/pack/documents_model.py`. The requirement editor moved in MIG-77 to `studio/pack/requirement_editor.py`. The demo requirement handler moved in MIG-78 to `studio/pack/requirement_demo.py`. The try-discovery file model moved in MIG-79 to `studio/claim/try_discovery_file.py`. The Claims model moved in MIG-80 to `studio/claim/claims_model.py`. The Discover model moved in MIG-81 to `studio/claim/discover_model.py`. The Check model moved in MIG-82 to `studio/check/check_model.py`. The try-discovery HTML moved in MIG-83 to `studio/claim/try_discovery.py`. The Discover page moved in MIG-84 to `studio/claim/discover_page.py`. The researcher HTML moved in MIG-85 to `studio/claim/researcher.py`. The pack-editor HTML moved in MIG-86 to `studio/pack/pages.py`. The legacy Publish page stayed in `requirements/ui/publication_page.py`. Extracted renderers (`app/cover.py`, `app/studio_page.py`, `app/scaffold_page.py`), the prepare POST (`app/prepare_post.py`), and the legacy Publish view-model (`app/publication_model.py`, including `_seal_selected` and `_publish_selected`), and the Home view-model (`app/home_model.py`, including `_select_pack`), and the Studio settings view-model (`app/studio_model.py`, including `_save_studio`), and the Review view-model (`app/review_model.py`, including `_compile_review`, `_save_review_decision`, and `_sign_review`), and the Scaffold write (`app/scaffold_write.py`) live beside `app/pages.py` until a later move. The Publish model is pending retirement under EP-11 and is not a new service. Shared chrome is still in `app/pages.py`. The target shell only starts the app and registers component routers.
2. **Fact.** Check execution is `checks/`, invoked by Studio as `run_and_write`. The target public API is `curbcheck/api.py`, with `studio/check/` as a thin adapter.
3. **Fact.** `review/compile.py` both assembles the report and records the human decision and signature. The target gives assembly and PDF to `studio/compile/` and the decision/signature to `studio/review/`.
4. **Fact.** `compile/pdf.py` is headless CLI only. Review's HTTP compile writes JSON, not PDF.
5. **Fact.** `claims/ui/http.py` encodes Check-picker rules (`exist`, `contents`, `fresh`, `owned`, `dep_ban`, and the legacy choice names) and writes Claims. That is domain behavior in the HTTP module.
6. **Fact.** `review/view_html.py` duplicates Review card markup and is unreferenced. Live markup is `app/pages.py`.
7. **Fact.** There is no authentication or authorization component. The only gate is "an open project", and it does not cover every POST.
8. **Fact.** Persistent validation is hand-written (`common/validate.py` and the extract/review validators). Packs v2 is not implemented as Pydantic, and JSON Schema is not generated from Pydantic.
9. **Fact.** Legacy Publish, demo `/requirement`, `/publish`, `/seal-pack`, `/prepare`, `/claims`, and `/scaffold` are still routed. Cover tests assert that `/claims` and `/scaffold` are not linked from Home. The handlers still exist.
10. **Fact.** `discovery/door.py` and `discovery/studio.py` hold workspace selection and Studio settings, inside a package whose other modules are Claim discovery.

## Missing test coverage

"Missing" means no assertion for that behavior was found. It does not mean the behavior is wrong.

**HTTP paths with no assertion:**

- GET `/vendor/jsondiffpatch.css`
- GET `/icons/curbpack-icon.svg`
- POST `/discover/claims/create` (`create_configured_claims` is tested directly)
- The literal "Unknown page." response

GET `/requirements/inspect` later moved in MIG-48. POST `/prepare` later moved in MIG-49. POST `/requirements/source` later moved in MIG-50. POST `/requirements/start-empty` later moved in MIG-51. The four paths still listed above are coverage gaps. They are not remaining handler extracts. GET `/vendor/jsondiffpatch.css` and GET `/icons/curbpack-icon.svg` are static files on the dispatcher. The literal unknown page is the final `_get` branch. POST `/discover/claims/create` is already outside `app/server.py`.

Step 1 remainder, reconciled against this register and `Handler._post` / `Handler._get`: seven POST branches had no handler test and three branches already had one. All ten later moved. Compare apply moved in MIG-55, consolidate apply in MIG-56, the extract job abort in MIG-57, the discover job abort in MIG-58, the demo save in MIG-59, the demo publish in MIG-60, and the demo pack seal in MIG-61. The discover run redirect moved in MIG-52, the demo requirement load in MIG-53, and the review page assembly in MIG-54.

**Pages with only a smoke assertion:** GET `/requirements/compare` and GET `/requirements/consolidate` (heading and no traceback, or redirect to the project list).

**Suites not in the baseline run:** `tests/cs/*` (path boundary, review signature, check-result integrity, git provenance, workspace selector). Native `make package*` was not run. `test_curbcheck_package.py` and `test_installers.py` did run as part of the 416; they mock or stop before a real host install. They are not a substitute for a packaged binary smoke test.

**Not claimed:** line coverage, branch coverage, or that every assertion in a long test (for example `test_rev03_review.py`) was re-executed against a reading of all 1,400 lines. The runner executed them. The matrix cites the assertions that identify each route.

## Proposed order to extract Python pages

**Items 1–7 are extracted.** Stage 1 page extraction for this list is complete. Each step moved one rendering function out of `app/pages.py`, left the URL and the handler in `app/server.py`, and kept `page`, `_flash`, `_reading`, `_current_project_label`, `progress_dialog`, and `repo_config_panel` importable from `app.pages`. `scaffold_page` also imports `page` and `_flash`. Do not combine the later handler slices. Do not start the next one after a failure.

Pages already in `requirements/ui/` and `claims/ui/` are not in this list. Splitting them again would not remove them from `app/pages.py`. Their server view-models follow this list, still one handler at a time.

## Decided ownership

Decided 2026-11-08. These four decisions are also named from [EP-02](EPIC.md). They do not authorize a new story, a package directory, or a behavior change in the current extract. None of them blocks the one-handler migration.

1. **Scaffold.** Its own Studio component, with its own page, created as late as possible. EP-02 preserves the current stub: `app/scaffold_page.py`, `app/scaffold_write.py`, `scaffold/stub.py`, and the `/scaffold` routes. MIG-29 extracts the existing POST. It does not create the component directory, agents, or Repair. EP-02 does not build a later agent or repair architecture. That later work may justify a separate repository. The component directory is created in that late slice, not before.
2. **Try-discovery.** Owned by `studio/claim/` Discovery. The requirement editor only makes an API call. The current button, file view, and job stay where they are until their own slice. Do not fold the search into `studio/pack/`.
3. **Authentication.** `studio/authentication/` and `studio/authorization/`, as in the target architecture. This tree still has no login beyond the open-project redirect. Adding those packages is not part of a behavior-preserving extract of an existing page.
4. **Compare and consolidate apply.** Owned by `studio/pack/`. One shared Requirements backend. The business logic does not live in the router or the UI. The missing HTTP posts were a recorded test gap, not an ownership gap. The compare post later moved in MIG-55. The consolidate post later moved in MIG-56.

## Open questions

1. **Project selection vs `studio/app.py`.** The target shell must not contain feature logic, and project selection is not a Pack, Claim, Check, Compile, or Review rule. Proposal: a small shell-adjacent module registered by `studio/app.py`, not an eighth product engine. Not decided.
2. **Unused `review/view_html.py`.** Proposal: do not migrate it. Confirm it is dead before any later deletion. Deletion is not part of MIG-00.
3. **Demo routes** `/requirement`, `/publish`, `/seal-pack`, `/prepare`. They are reachable and partly untested over HTTP. Proposal: preserve them as historical behavior under `studio/pack/` when their pages are extracted, and do not treat them as the Pack editor. Not a decision to remove them.
4. **`tests/cs/` not collected by `make test`.** Whether they should be added to the suite is a test-policy decision, not a migration step.

## MIG-18 — extract the Discover packs GET model

**Status:** extract gate passed. Move, separate, Pydantic, FastAPI, and TypeScript are not this step.

| Field | Record |
| --- | --- |
| Identifier | Discover packs. GET `/discover` and GET `/discover/repo` show the pack list for the open project. `/discover/repo` passes `product=True`, which only sets `mode` to `product`. |
| Baseline | Parent revision `5113d9e4e95a21a068442e1c24590326636f4d29`. GET model was `app/server.py` `_discover_model`. The URL stayed in `Handler._get`. |
| Extract | `app/discover_model.py` `_discover_model` and `_discover_pack_rows`. Same return value and the same calls, apart from importing `_listed_repos`, `_sync_open_repo`, and `_door_path_fields` from `app.server` when the model runs. Those three stay because other handlers use them. `_discover_pack_rows` moved because nothing else called it. `_discover_sections`, `_curbpack_pack_ids`, `_copy_choices`, and `_copy_claim_counts` stay in `app/server.py` because this model does not call them. The model calls `_sync_open_repo`. When `repo` or `repo_id` names an already registered id, that writes `open-repo.json`. `Handler.do_GET` calls `_sync_open_repo` before the page as well, so a registered id is written twice and the page returns. An unknown id raises `ModelError` before that write. `do_GET` discards the first raise. `/discover` and `/discover/repo` require an open project. With no open repo, `do_GET` redirects to `/project` and the model does not run. That project page calls `ensure_repos_populated`, which writes `product_repos.json` only when the repo list is empty and a child directory has `curbpack.json`. An existing repo list is returned without that write. With an open repo and an unknown id, the model's second call raises and `do_GET` calls `_fail`. A registered id, and a query with no repo id while a repo is already open, do not raise. A query with no repo id skips the `open-repo.json` write. An already open repo can still rewrite a retired claim. `_discover_pack_rows` reads `curbpack.json` and pack requirements. A claim that already has `checks` is only read. A stored claim is rewritten in place only when `check` is a non-empty string, `id` and `requirement_id` are strings, and `validate_claim` accepts the upgraded document: `read_claims` calls `load_claim`, and `load_claim` writes that claim. `upgrade_stored_claim` calls that shape retired. Otherwise the file stays. `ModelError` from that read is swallowed and the row builder uses no claims. `read_discover_job` and `reviewable_targets` only read. `app/server.py` still owns both URLs. POST `/discover/repos` and POST `/discover/run` stay there. `discover_page` stayed in `claims/ui/pages.py`. `reviewable_targets` is no longer imported by `app/server.py`. `read_discover_job` stays because `_send_discover_job` uses it. No `studio/` directory. |

## MIG-23 — extract the Review GET model

**Status:** extract gate passed. Move, separate, Pydantic, FastAPI, and TypeScript are not this step.

| Field | Record |
| --- | --- |
| Identifier | Review. GET `/review` shows the review board when a project is open. |
| Baseline | Parent revision `f4bcf759507b8bc2e6cd98c578897dfdbb7b877b`. GET model was `app/server.py` `_review_view`. The URL stayed in `Handler._get`. `review_page` was already in `app/review_page.py`. |
| Extract | `app/review_model.py` `_review_view`, `_review_file_view`, `_dated_entries`, `_same_as_latest_run`, `_expanded_query`, `_raw_presentation`, `_report_presentation`, and `_unchecked_for_repo`. Same return values and the same calls, apart from importing `_sync_open_repo`, `_registered_repo_path`, `_requested_dated`, and `_report_sources_for_repo` from `app.server` when they run. `_requested_dated` and `_report_sources_for_repo` stay because POST `/review/compile` uses them. `_sync_open_repo` and `_registered_repo_path` stay because other handlers use them. `load_report`, `read_report_signature`, `same_run`, and `unchecked_requirement_ids` are no longer imported by `app/server.py`. The model calls `_sync_open_repo`. When `repo` or `repo_id` names an already registered id, that writes `open-repo.json`. `Handler.do_GET` calls `_sync_open_repo` before the page. After a normal return, `Handler._get` calls `_door_chrome`, which calls `_sync_open_repo` again, so a registered id that renders the page is written three times. An unknown id raises `ModelError` before that write. `do_GET` discards the first raise. `/review` requires an open project. With no open repo, `do_GET` redirects to `/project` and the model does not run. That project page calls `ensure_repos_populated`, which writes `product_repos.json` only when the repo list is empty and a child directory has `curbpack.json`. An existing repo list is returned without that write. With an open repo and an unknown id, the model's call raises. The message does not contain `curbpack.json`, so the handler re-raises and `do_GET` calls `_fail`. A query with no repo id skips the `open-repo.json` write. With an open repo, `_get` calls `_registered_sources` before the page. That calls `ensure_catalog`, which writes `registered_sources.json` only when that file is missing. An existing catalog is read and not rewritten. The redirect returns before `_get`, so it does not seed the catalog. A `ModelError` or `ValueError` whose text contains `curbpack.json` is caught in `Handler._get`. That path calls `_sync_open_repo` again and renders the error page. A selected report calls `_report_presentation`, and a selected result calls `_raw_presentation`. Each calls `load_claims`. A claim that already has `checks` is only read. A stored claim is rewritten in place only when `check` is a non-empty string, `id` and `requirement_id` are strings, and `validate_claim` accepts the upgraded document. Otherwise the file stays. A GET with neither `report` nor `result` does not call `load_claims`. `_unchecked_for_repo` reads pack directories and does not write. `_review_file_view` reads one repo-relative file and does not write. `git_commit_signature` runs `git log` and does not write. POST `/review`, `/review/compile`, and the sign branch stay in `app/server.py`. `/review/compile` calls `write_report`. The sign branch calls `save_report_signature`. `app/server.py` still owns the URL and `_door_chrome`. `review_page` stayed in `app/review_page.py`. No `studio/` directory. |

An unknown id raises `ModelError` before that write. `do_GET` discards the first raise. `/claims` is not behind the open-project redirect. With no open repo, the model still runs. Claims GET has no catch for that error, so `do_GET` calls `_fail` and the claims page is not rendered. When the claims directory exists, `load_claims` runs before the prefill check. A claim that already has `checks` is only read. A stored claim is rewritten in place only when `check` is a non-empty string, `id` and `requirement_id` are strings, and `validate_claim` accepts the upgraded document. Otherwise the file stays. An unknown `requirement_id` raises `ModelError` after that read, so a rejected prefill can already have rewritten a retired claim. `load_run` reads the latest run result and does not write. It swallows `ModelError`, `OSError`, and `ValueError` and returns `None`. `read_claim_seal` reads a seal or runs `git log` and does not write. `exist_args_for_path` is called only when the prefill path is non-empty, and its return value is discarded. It does not write. `_door_path_fields` reads `curbpack.json` and does not write it. POST `/claims`, `/claims/{id}`, `/claims/{id}/sign`, and `/claims/{id}/retire` stay in `app/server.py`. Those POST routes call `write_claim`, `retire_claim`, or `sign_claim`. The saved/cleared/signed/retired message stayed in `app/server.py`. `claims_page` stayed in `app/claims_page.py`. ## MIG-33 — extract the Review sign POST

**Status:** extract gate passed. Move, separate, Pydantic, FastAPI, and TypeScript are not this step. This extract does not make `app/server.py` a thin dispatcher.

| Field | Record |
| --- | --- |
| Identifier | Review. POST `/review/sign` seals one report and redirects to `/review?signed=1`. |
| Baseline | Parent revision `e6ff1c46c366972c1ee48854e080e61e06c83717`. The branch was inline in `Handler._post`. The URL stayed there. |
| Extract | `app/review_model.py` `_sign_review`. Same calls, apart from importing `_now`, `_registered_repo_path`, and `_sync_open_repo` from `app.server` when it runs. Those three stay because other handlers use them. `_confined_review_report_path` moved into this module because `_save_review_decision` was the only other caller. `_save_review_decision` now calls that local function. The function returns the URL that the branch passed to `self._redirect`. `Handler._post` redirects to that return value. `save_report_signature` and `reports_dir` are no longer imported by `app/server.py`. `load_or_create_key` stays imported because other handlers use it. The function calls `_sync_open_repo`. `Handler.do_POST` calls it once before the branch. When `repo` or `repo_id` names an already registered id, that writes `open-repo.json` once, and the function writes it again. An unknown id raises `ModelError` before either write. `do_POST` discards the first raise. With no open repo left, `do_POST` redirects to `/project` and the function does not run. `load_or_create_key` does not run. With a repo already open, the function raises again. The object name is `open-repo.json`, which does not start with `requirement `, so `do_POST` calls `_fail`. No report is sealed. A form with no repo id skips both writes. When a repo is already open, the function uses that id. If that second call returns no id, the function raises `ModelError` with object name `review` before `load_or_create_key`. `/review/sign` requires an open project. A posted registered id is opened before the gate, so the function runs. With no open repo and no posted registered id, the redirect returns before `_post` and does not seed the catalog. That project page calls `ensure_repos_populated`, which writes `product_repos.json` only when the repo list is empty and a child directory has `curbpack.json`. An existing repo list is returned without that write. With an open repo, `Handler._post` calls `claim_http.handle_post` with `sources=_registered_sources(ws)` before the branch. That argument is evaluated for every POST `/review/sign` that reaches `_post`. `_registered_sources` calls `ensure_catalog`, which writes `registered_sources.json` only when that file is missing. An existing catalog is read and not rewritten. `handle_post` returns `None` for this URL. `trust_store` is evaluated for that same call. It reads `keys.json` when that file exists and does not create it. The function reads the registered repo, not `Workspace.repo`, unless that row resolves to `root / "product"`. A missing `curbpack.json` raises `FileNotFoundError` after the open-repo write and before `load_or_create_key`. Invalid JSON raises `ValueError` from `read_json`. A schema-invalid file raises `ModelError` from `validate_curbpack_config`. `do_POST` calls `_fail` for all three. `ModelError` is shown as the exception text. `FileNotFoundError` and `ValueError` become the generic error page. `_confined_review_report_path` moved here because this function and `_save_review_decision` were the only callers. It returns an empty string only when the reports directory has no `review-report_*.json` file. A requested path that is outside that directory, or is not such a file, falls through to the newest matching file. An empty return raises `ModelError` with object name `review` before `load_or_create_key`, so no new key is written. `load_or_create_key` creates the keys directory when it is missing. An existing name is returned and `keys.json` is not rewritten. A name that is not already stored calls `generate_keypair` before `write_json`. When cryptography is missing, that raises `SigningUnavailable` inside `load_or_create_key`, `keys.json` is not written, and `sign_object` is not called. When the name is new and cryptography is present, `keys.json` is written before `save_report_signature`. An existing name is returned before `write_json`. `SigningUnavailable` for that existing name is raised by `_require_signer` inside `sign_object`, after `load_or_create_key` and before `write_report`. `SigningUnavailable` is not a `ModelError`. `do_POST` calls `_fail`, and `_fail` shows that exception text. An empty name is passed through to `load_or_create_key`. `save_report_signature` reads the existing file. A missing file raises `ModelError` with object name `review report` before `write_report`. If a new key was already written, that key file stays and the report is not sealed. `load_report` calls `validate_report` before `sign_report`. A blank `decision` already in the file raises `ModelError` with object name `review report` before `sign_report`. A missing `decision` key reaches `sign_report`, which raises `ModelError` expected `words recorded before review-sign`. A missing `run_result` fails in `validate_run_result` with object name `run result` before `sign_report`. All three happen before `write_report`. An empty name reaches `_require_signer`, which raises `ModelError` with object name `signed` before `write_report`. `sign_report` only builds the sealed document in memory. `write_report` overwrites that same path. It does not create a new dated file. `SigningUnavailable` is not a `ModelError`. `do_POST` calls `_fail`, and `_fail` shows that exception text. That raise is inside `sign_object`, after `load_or_create_key`. The function does not write `curbpack.json` or a claim file. `load_or_create_key` stays imported by `app/server.py` because other handlers use it. `reports_dir` and `save_report_signature` are no longer imported by `app/server.py`. `_now` stays in `app/server.py`. This extract does not make `app/server.py` a thin dispatcher. This extract adds no file under `studio/`. |

If `pack.json` is written and `_write_curbpack` then raises before its replace, the new `pack.json` stays and the previous `curbpack.json` stays. Update writes the existing `pack.json` only. It does not write `curbpack.json`. A pack that is not named in `curbpack.json` raises before that write. `manual` clears the URL. Another adapter that does not match the URL raises before the write. `write_json` creates the parent directory when it is missing, writes a temporary file, and replaces the destination. If that write raises before the replace, the temporary file is removed and an existing file stays. Neither path writes `.env`. `make_document_id` is no longer imported by `app/server.py`. GET `_documents_model` stays. This extract does not make `app/server.py` a thin dispatcher. This extract adds no file under `studio/`. |

Same calls, apart from importing `_adapter_for_url` and `_locate_repo_pack` from `app.server` when `_add_pack_section` runs. Those two stay because other handlers use them. The function returns the URL that the branch passed to `self._redirect`. `Handler._post` redirects to that return value. `find_document`, `add_section`, `save_catalog`, and `make_id` are no longer imported by `app/server.py`. The function does not call `_sync_open_repo`. `Handler.do_POST` calls it once before the branch. A registered `repo` or `repo_id` writes `open-repo.json` once. The function does not write it again. An unknown id raises `ModelError` before that write. `do_POST` discards the raise. `/requirements/catalog` does not require an open project, so the function still runs. A form with neither `repo` nor `repo_id` skips that write. `Handler._post` calls `claim_http.handle_post` with `sources=_registered_sources(ws)` before the branch. That argument is evaluated for every POST `/requirements/catalog` that reaches `_post`. `_registered_sources` calls `ensure_catalog`, which writes `registered_sources.json` only when that file is missing. An existing catalog is read and not rewritten. `handle_post` returns `None` for this URL. `trust_store` is evaluated for that same call. That call, and the earlier call from `_registered_sources`, can seed a missing catalog before a later `ModelError`. `_add_pack_section` calls `ensure_catalog` only after a non-empty section id, a located pack, an adapter, and an extractable section. An empty section id, an unknown repo, a missing pack, a missing adapter, or a section that cannot be extracted raises before that call. `find_document` chooses the branch. A catalog document calls `add_section` and then `save_catalog`. `add_section` raises `ModelError` with object name `catalog` before `save_catalog` when the section is empty, not extractable, or already present. The seed from `ensure_catalog` can already be on disk. A pack id calls `_add_pack_section`. An empty section id raises `ModelError` with object name `pack` before `_locate_repo_pack`. `_locate_repo_pack` reads the registered repo, not `Workspace.repo`, unless that row resolves to `root / "product"`. An unknown repo id raises `ModelError` with object name `pack` after the function's `ensure_catalog`. Invalid JSON in `curbpack.json` is `ValueError` from `read_json` before a catalog source is appended, and `_fail` shows the generic error page. `validate_curbpack_config` rejects a bad document with object name `curbpack.json` before the pack lookup returns. A missing pack, a missing adapter, or a section that cannot be extracted raises `ModelError` with object name `pack` before `save_catalog`. An existing source row for that pack and section returns without `save_catalog`. A new row calls `save_catalog`, which writes `registered_sources.json` through `write_json`. `write_json` creates the parent directory when it is missing, writes a temporary file, and replaces the destination. If that write raises before the replace, the temporary file is removed and an existing catalog stays. The function does not write a Requirement, `pack.json`, or `curbpack.json`. `find_document`, `add_section`, `save_catalog`, and `make_id` are no longer imported by `app/server.py`. `ensure_catalog` stays imported because `_registered_sources` uses it. `make_id` stays imported by `app/catalog_model.py` because the GET model also uses it. `_locate_repo_pack` and `_adapter_for_url` stay in `app/server.py`. GET `_catalog_model` stays. This extract does not make `app/server.py` a thin dispatcher. This extract adds no file under `studio/`. |

Those raises happen before `save_repos`, so `product_repos.json` is not written. `save_repos` then writes `product_repos.json`. `set_open_repo` then writes `open-repo.json` when the new id is non-empty. `_safe_local_next` keeps a same-origin relative path. A `next` value that does not start with `/`, starts with `//`, or contains a backslash or a newline is discarded, and the function returns `/discover?added=1`. A kept `next` that does not already contain `try_repo=` gains `try_repo=` and the quoted new id when that id is non-empty. The function does not write `curbpack.json`. `load_repos`, `add_repo`, `save_repos`, and `set_open_repo` stay imported by `app/server.py` because other handlers use them. `_safe_local_next` stays because another handler uses it. This extract does not make `app/server.py` a thin dispatcher. This extract adds no file under `studio/`. |

| Baseline | Parent revision `3bba38b7f83d48c5b37898d1a2f10f687c0337e8`. `_start_discover` and `_start_pack_discover` were in `app/server.py`. The URL stayed in `Handler._post`. |

That helper stays because other handlers use it. The function still returns the job id. `Handler._post` still redirects to `/discover?job=` plus that id. The function does not call `_sync_open_repo` or `set_open_repo`. `Handler.do_POST` calls `_sync_open_repo` once before the branch. A registered `repo` or `repo_id` writes `open-repo.json` once. An unknown id raises `ModelError` before that write. `do_POST` discards the raise. `/discover/run` requires an open project. With no open repo, and no registered id in the form, `do_POST` redirects to `/project` and the function does not run. That project page calls `ensure_repos_populated`, which writes `product_repos.json` only when the repo list is empty and a child directory has `curbpack.json`. A registered posted id opens the project before that gate, so the function still runs. `Handler._post` calls `claim_http.handle_post` with `sources=_registered_sources(ws)` before the branch. That argument is evaluated for every POST `/discover/run` that reaches `_post`. `_registered_sources` calls `ensure_catalog`, which writes `registered_sources.json` only when that file is missing. `handle_post` returns `None` for this URL. `trust_store` is evaluated for that call and reads `keys.json` when the file exists. It does not create it. A non-empty `pack_id` uses `_start_pack_discover`. An unknown repo raises `ModelError` before `curbpack.json` is read and before a job file. Invalid `curbpack.json` raises before the pack-membership check. A pack id that is not in that file raises before `repo_pack_requirements` and before a job file. A non-digit `assess_limit` raises before `start_discover_job`. A limit outside 0 to 20 raises inside `start_discover_job` before `jobs` is created. The source path, with no `pack_id`, calls `_registered_sources` again after the repo row is found. An unknown source, a source with no pack id, or an empty timestamp raises before `resolve_copy_ref`. `resolve_copy_ref` only reads the named copy. A missing copy raises before `curbpack.json` is read and before a job file. `start_discover_job` creates `jobs` if needed and writes a running job file. It then calls `set_repo_status`, which rewrites `product_repos.json` with `last_status` `running`, and only then starts the daemon thread. The redirect returns after that start. The thread can later update the job and rewrite `product_repos.json` to `done`, `aborted`, or `failed`. The function returns the job id. The handler redirects to `/discover?job=` plus that id. The function does not write `curbpack.json` or the working copy. `CURBPACK_COPY` and `start_discover_job` are no longer imported by `app/server.py`. An empty `adapter_id` is HTTP 400 and does not create `source_adapter.json`. `missing-adapter` is HTTP 400 and leaves `source_adapter.json` with that id. `cra` redirects to `/requirements/inspect?adapter=cra` and leaves that id in the file. |
| Extract | `app/inspect_model.py` `_select_source`. Same calls, apart from importing `_load_adapter` from `app.server` when it runs. That helper stays because other handlers use it. The function returns the URL that the branch passed to `self._redirect`. `Handler._post` redirects to that return value. The function does not call `_sync_open_repo`. `Handler.do_POST` calls it once before the branch. A registered `repo` or `repo_id` writes `open-repo.json` once. An unknown id raises `ModelError` before that write. `do_POST` discards the raise. `/requirements/source` does not require an open project, so the function still runs. A form with neither `repo` nor `repo_id` skips that write. `Handler._post` calls `claim_http.handle_post` with `sources=_registered_sources(ws)` before the branch. That argument is evaluated for every POST `/requirements/source` that reaches `_post`. `_registered_sources` calls `ensure_catalog`, which writes `registered_sources.json` only when that file is missing. `handle_post` returns `None` for this URL. `trust_store` is evaluated for that call and reads `keys.json` when the file exists. It does not create it. An empty `adapter_id` makes `mark_source_adapter` raise `ModelError` before `source_adapter.json` is written and before `_load_adapter`. An unknown id writes `source_adapter.json` and then `_load_adapter` raises `ModelError`. That file remains. A known id writes `source_adapter.json`, loads the adapter, and the function returns `/requirements/inspect?adapter=` plus the id. The id is not quoted. The function does not write `pack.json`, `curbpack.json`, or a requirement. `mark_source_adapter` stays imported by `app/server.py` because another handler uses it. `_load_adapter` stays there. ## Stop

MIG-114 stops here. Stage 2 remains stopped at MIG-86 for the already separated modules. The legacy Publish page stays outside `studio/`. Cover stays in `claims/ui/`. Studio settings and Home stay out of `studio/app.py`. Scaffold's component is not created. The Publish model stays in `app/publication_model.py`. `claims/ui/http.py` stays until a router exists. `checks/__main__.py` stays on the engine until the CLI lives in `curbcheck/cli.py`. `compile-pdf.py` stays the PDF command. `review/gitseal.py` stays the shared signature reading. The Review POST adapters stay in `app/review_model.py`. The remaining stage 3 modules are `requirements/workspace.py`, `requirements/compare.py`, `requirements/consolidate.py`, `requirements/jobs.py`, `requirements/extract/`, `claims/bind.py`, the Discovery domain, and `claims/ui/coverage.py`.
